*Scarcely half a month has passed since the new term began and I became a second-year.*
*And yet this has been perhaps the most eventful fifteen days of my entire life.*
*It all started at the festival.*
*A while back, there was this anime called "Mobius Ring, the Cycle of Reincarnation".*
*The town I live in, Shiromitsugawa, helped sponsor it as a way of promoting tourism to the area.*
*The show had some convoluted plot based off of local folklore. It left a big impression on a small group of dedicated fans, but most people regarded it as one of those oddball shows that doesn't make a whole lot of sense.*
*Though it went down in anime history as a complete failure, it's unclear whether the bigwigs behind the regional sponsorship deal are clueless to that fact or simply aren't willing to admit it.*
*It's been two years since the anime aired.*
*And so we had the second annual Mobius Festival, a hamfisted attempt to get some mileage out of the anime despite the local residents' overwhelming opposition to going through the cringeworthy event a second time.*
*Unfortunately, an earthquake happened right as things were getting started, putting an abrupt end to the festivities.*
*To make matters worse, the earthquake shattered the namesake treasure of the Shrine of the White Serpent's Relic, a location featured prominently in the anime, and also the site of the festival.*
*As a result, a gate opened between our world and a parallel dimension.*
*I wish I was joking.*
*But no, that really happened.*
*After that, we found a statue that turned out to be a petrified human. That was the start of my surreal half-month.*
*The next major incident was when one of the students from my school, Hakusen Academy, set the building ablaze using some strange power.*
*During the fray, my classmate Miyako Kujo used some kind of superpower to extinguish the source of the flames.*
*And if that all wasn't crazy enough, one of the residents of this parallel world, Sophitia, showed up at my house.*
*After seeing so many crazy things, all I could do was believe the story she told me.*
*It appeared all of these incidents had been caused by powerful objects known as Artifacts. They spilled into our world from Sophitia's, but they look like ordinary jewelry.*
*They grant their wielders immense power, but are also liable to drive them insane.*
*Not even Sophitia knows how many Artifacts have found their way to our world.*
*We still haven't caught the murderer behind the petrification incidents, and there's a real possibility that other malicious Users with no reservations about utilizing their power might pop up.*
*If that happens, there'll be even more innocent victims.*
*Worse, if other villains appear, they might spur the User of the Evil Eye to commit even greater atrocities.*
*I've teamed up with Kujo to catch the culprit, and our first course of action has been to look for trustworthy Users to win over to our cause.*
*I figured searching for allies would take some time, but to my surprise we found another User pretty easily.*
*Turns out my sister Sora, of all people, is a User too.*
*I never expected someone that close to me would be wrapped up in all this. Getting another comrade so quickly was kinda anticlimactic, really.*
*Anyway, after recruiting my sister we started checking the fan forums for the Mobius Ring anime to see if any real Users had been posting there. Eventually we ran across someone who looked like they fit the bill.*
*Haruka Kosaka.*
*The third-year from my school who's always surrounded by a harem of dudes.*
*Her popularity's unnatural, and her overbearing, regal demeanor is pretty similar to this person called Queen of Eden we found on the forums.*
*We thought there was a pretty good chance she was another User, so we decided to investigate her.*
*Unfortunately, things didn't go quite as planned.*
*Sora, whose ability is perfect for covert ops, offered to tail Kosaka for us. But then she got caught by Kosaka's comrades.*
*They introduced themselves as Rig Veda, and invited us to join their organization.*
*Their leader is a guy called Commander.*
*And his goal is to create a paradise for Users.*
*He said that in order to achieve that goal he was willing to kill, if necessary.*
*Naturally there was no way I'd cooperate with a guy like that, so I refused his invitation.*
*He didn't like that one bit.*
Commander: "I see. In that case—You three are our enemy."
*And thus, he declared war on us.*
*It was a dicey situation.*
*None of our Artifacts were suited for fighting.*
*But we couldn't afford to let a man as dangerous as Commander run free.*
*Just as we'd resolved to fight—*
???: "So you employ violence against those who refuse to join you. How barbaric. I can't believe you have the gall to call yourselves superior to humanity."
*An unexpected ally appeared.*
*Nine Ball's Parfait Queen, Noa Yuuki.*
*Turns out she was a User and, just like us, she didn't agree with Rig Veda's ideals or methods.*
*She engaged Commander in the most cringey powerlevel debate I've ever seen. The ridiculous chuuni argument helped dispel the tension between our two groups though, and Rig Veda left without a fight.*
Ghost: "You better watch your back, onii-chan."
*That was the parting remark one of Rig Veda's members left for me as she departed.*
*Unwilling to be outdone on the chuuni scale, Yuuki went on to create the Valhalla Society to fight Rig Veda, and we ended up becoming a part of it.*
*We're pretty certain the Evil Eye's User, the one who turned that poor girl to stone, is one of Rig Veda's members.*
*As a fellow User, it's my responsibility to stop them.*
*My resolve renewed, I go to bed.*

*Everything from her style of speech to the tone of her voice is entirely unlike Ms. Naruse.*
*I always figured gods were the things of myths, but now we're staring right at one.*
Iris: "Feel free to relax. It will take a long time to explain everything."
Kakeru: "..."
Haruka: "..."
*Senpai and I exchange glances.*
*I relax my posture, but Senpai keeps her back ramrod straight, just like Iris.*
*Seemingly satisfied with Senpai's choice, Iris smiles in approval and gives her a little nod.*
Iris: "I imagine there is a lot you wish to ask of me, but let me first explain my circumstances up until now."
Iris: "I imagine my story will answer at least a few of your questions."
Kakeru: "...Sure."
Iris: "Well then..."
*Iris's smile grows wider, and she begins her tale.*
*Unsurprisingly, she starts with the events of a thousand years ago.*
*Originally, our world and Iris's world were connected.*
*Back then, Iris was close friends with Ms. Naruse's ancestors, though she made sure not to interfere in the affairs of our world too much.*
*But things changed drastically when the people of Iris's world invented Artifacts.*
Haruka: "Does this mean Artifacts are tools created by human hands?"
Iris: "Correct. Before their invention, people used... I suppose we can call it magic. There were a few people who could wield special powers without the aid of an Artifact."
Iris: "However, mastering magic required years of rigorous study, and only those with innate talent could use it."
Haruka: "So Artifacts were developed to allow everyone to use magic?"
Iris: "Yes. Even so, not everyone can wield an Artifact."
Kakeru: "Because Artifacts choose their masters."
Iris: "As a result, many of the Artifacts we created ended up crossing over into this world."
*That was when disaster struck.*
*Just like how when Artifacts started reappearing here a few weeks ago, the school nearly burned down.*
*Or how Yoichi's going around killing people.*
*The people who were swallowed up by their own powers caused a calamity in Shiromitsugawa.*
*And it's the person sitting in front of us, Iris, who put an end to their rampage.*
Iris: "After I recovered all the Artifacts that had spilled over, I left the twin to my World's Eye behind in this world."
Kakeru: "It has a twin?"
Iris: "Yes. Everyone has two eyes, do they not?"
*Smiling, Iris points to her own eyes.*
Iris: "The World's Eyes are like a door. They can open pathways, as well as lock them shut."
Iris: "By placing one eye in each world and then closing both of their eyelids, I was able to sever the connection between them—or so I thought."
Kakeru: "But then, the eye in this world got damaged."
Iris: "Correct. One eye was opened, and that served as a aperture for Artifacts to flood into this world once more."
Iris: "All of this is my fault. Mine, and everyone else from my world's. I'm terribly sorry for all of the suffering we have caused you."
Iris: "Despite what I said, my goal is no different from Sophitia's. I wish to reclaim the Artifacts, and I would like your help in doing so."
Iris: "However, I prefer to use peaceful methods."
Iris: "That is my desire. It is also why I contacted you two."
Haruka: "..."
Haruka: "I don't understand."
*Senpai mutters suddenly.*
*Looking resolutely at Iris, she asks the question that's been on my mind as well.*
Haruka: "Why haven't you come to this world personally to resolve this crisis like you did a thousand years ago?"
Haruka: "Compared to you, we're utter novices when it comes to using Artifacts."
Haruka: "Yet you want to rely on us. I cannot possibly fathom why."
Iris: "During the incident a thousand years ago, I didn't visit this world in person either."
Haruka: "Huh?"
Iris: "To be more precise, I wasn't able to."
Haruka: "But you just said you recovered the Artifacts from this world."
Iris: "Yes, I did. I borrowed the body of someone from the Naruse family, just as I am doing now."
*She places a hand on her—Ms. Naruse's chest.*
Iris: "Living beings cannot jump between worlds. Even with the power of the World's Eye, I cannot physically transport myself into your world."
Iris: "Which was why I sent only my soul over last time. I borrowed the body of the woman who shared my soul resonance, and used my powers through her."
Haruka: "Your soul resonance?"
Iris: "Everyone has a counterpart in each other's world who shares the same soul as them. Though they may be a man in one world and a woman in the other, their souls are identical."
Iris: "Kakeru-san, Haruka-san, you two also possess doppelgangers living in another world who share your soul."
Iris: "We refer to those whose souls are linked as Resonants."
Kakeru: "Does that mean Ms. Naruse is..."
Iris: "Your assumption is correct. Every generation, the Naruse family gives birth to one person who is my Resonant."
Iris: "Satsuki just happens to be this generation's Resonant."
Kakeru: "So you can only borrow the body of your Resonant?"
Iris: "Yes. I can only inhabit someone's body by aligning my soul's wavelength with theirs. Meaning I cannot possess anyone other than Satsuki."
Haruka: "I'm afraid that doesn't answer my question. If you didn't have a Resonant I could see why you might need someone else's help, but you're talking to us through her right now, aren't you?"
Haruka: "So why not use your own power to resolve things?"
Iris: "...Because I cannot."
*For the first time, Iris's expression clouds over.*
Iris: "After a thousand years, my power has weakened considerably."
*She casts her gaze downward and squeezes her hands together.*
Iris: "I cannot synchronize with my Resonant as well as I used to. Even if I could, I can no longer use Artifacts with the same proficiency."
Haruka: "Which is why you have no choice but to ask us for help?"
Iris: "As shameful as it is, I'm afraid so."
*She smiles weakly.*
*I see. If we can believe everything Iris is saying, that would explain why Sophie's communicating with us through a stuffed animal.*
*She can't physically cross over into this world either.*
*Instead, she sent over an illusory body that she then took over by synchronizing to it like a Resonant.*
*That's probably the "irregular usage" of her illusory body that she was referring to a while back.*
*Just like Iris, she can't use her Artifacts here because either she can't synchronize to her body perfectly, or the body itself is just too weak.*
*Which is why she needs our help.*
*But... hang on a second.*
Kakeru: "Isn't... Sophie the one who owns the World's Eye right now?"
Iris: "Yes. I used to own the Evil Eye as well."
Haruka: "By used to, I assume you mean..."
Iris: "I became unable to control it, and I relinquished ownership of it. I see now that was a mistake."
Kakeru: "Are you working against Sophie?"
Iris: "The situation is a bit more complicated than that, but... I suppose for simplicity's sake you could look at it that way."
Iris: "Has she told you about Sephirot?"
Kakeru: "Yeah. She said it was an organization that manages Artifacts."
Iris: "That's correct. It's an organization I founded to ensure the tragedy that occurred a thousand years ago never happens again. However... like all ancient organizations, it has changed over time."
Iris: "Not all of Sephirot's current members believe in our original goal."
Haruka: "So there's an internal rift within the organization?"
Iris: "Embarrassing though it is to admit, yes. There are those among us who believe Artifacts should not be locked away but rather used for the benefit of the world."
Kakeru: "I'm guessing Sophie's in that camp?"
Iris: "Yes. Sophitia plans to use the Artifacts she collects to further her own goals. All while claiming she's doing it for the greater good."
Iris: "That is the reason you absolutely must not hand over any Artifacts to her. Misuse of Artifacts will only bring about another tragedy."
Iris: "I beseech you, please..."
Iris: "..."
Iris: "I'm tired."
Kakeru: "Huh?"
*That came out of nowhere. For a second Senpai and I just look at her in utter confusion.*
*Iris lets out a languid sigh, her dignified expression vanishing.*
Iris: "Man, can I stop talking like this already?"
Kakeru: "Hm? Uhh, what?"
Iris: "I figured since everyone thinks I'm a god I should try and act the part, but now my shoulders are getting stiff. Acting all dignified and solemn really isn't for me. God, I'm pooped."
Kakeru: "..."
Haruka: "..."
*Dumbfounded, Senpai and I watch as Iris casually rolls her shoulders and stretches her back.*
*She looks... exactly like Ms. Naruse, actually. Not divine at all.*
*So this is Ms. Naruse's Resonant.*
*It didn't really click with me at first, but now I feel like I get what Iris was talking about.*
*She, uhh, is a lot like Ms. Naruse, that's for sure. I can see the similarities now.*
Iris: "Why'd everyone start revering me as a god, anyway? Until the incident with the Artifacts, I was just like, their pen pal."
Haruka: "Well... I imagine they felt indebted to you after you saved them."
Iris: "Can you really say I saved them when it's our fault the Artifacts ended up in this world in the first place? It's not like I could just ignore what was going on."
Iris: "But, well, I guess it's not so bad, being worshiped. In my world I'm just an old dotard."
Kakeru: "Oh yeah, I missed my timing to ask, but... you've been alive for a thousand years, right?"
Iris: "Give or take a few."
Kakeru: "Does that mean you're immortal?"
Iris: "I don't age, but I'm not immortal. I've just stopped my body from getting any older."
Haruka: "I assume that's the power of an Artifact—or is it magic?"
Iris: "No one uses magic these days. Artifacts are just way more convenient. I made an Artifact that stops my aging."
Iris: "But even if my body's young, my soul's ancient. I'm a lot weaker than I used to be."
Iris: "Now that I've lost most of my powers I don't have any real authority left either. I'm basically just Sephirot's figurehead."
Iris: "That's why Sophitia and the others can get away with doing whatever they want."
Iris: "I'm pretty sure you and the Evil Eye's User aren't the only people Sophitia's got cooperating with her. There's gotta be more Users in the city that she's in touch with."
Iris: "She's probably changing her approach based on who she's working with, too. Like she's being friendly to you guys and advocating for the peaceful solution, but she's probably egging the Evil Eye's User on to kill more people."
Iris: "She's just using all of you to get her hands on your Artifacts. Well, I'm using you guys too so I guess I'm not one to talk."
Haruka: "You don't want the faction pushing for more proactive use of Artifacts to gain any more traction, I take it?"
Iris: "You got it. But I can't do anything directly, which is why I want you guys' help."
*She holds her hand out to us, as if offering something. But her palm is empty.*
*A second later, though, the space above her hand warps in the same way it does when Sophie appears, and an ornately decorated vial materializes in it.*
Kakeru: "What's that?"
Iris: "Ambrosia. It's a medicine that lets you annul someone's contract with their Artifact."
Haruka: "When you mentioned earlier that you relinquished your Artifact I was curious how you managed that, but now I see."
Iris: "Yeah, this is how. You guys are having trouble finding a way to stop the Evil Eye's User, right?"
Kakeru: "...Yeah."
Iris: "This should solve your problems. If you can inject even a little of this into his bloodstream, or make him drink it, you'll be able to render him powerless."
Iris: "You'll be able to retrieve his Artifact without a fight. Simple, right?"
Kakeru: "Is it that simple?"
Haruka: "I suspect we shall need to fight him in order to force him to drink this."
Iris: "Well, it's your job to figure out how you're gonna do that. Anyway, here."
Kakeru: "Whoa!"
*I barely manage to catch the vial before it hits the ground.*
*Man, what a careless throw.*
*Isn't this like, super valuable?*
Iris: "Take it. You guys decide whether or not you want to use it, and if so, how."
Iris: "I know you can't trust me yet, so I won't blame you if you decide not to use it at all, either."
Iris: "Everything's in your hands. That includes the Artifacts you guys recover... All I can do is ask that you hand them over to me."
Iris: "However... if giving me those Artifacts would put your lives at risk, then I'd rather you give them to Sophitia."
Iris: "Your safety takes priority over all else. My goal isn't nearly as important as your lives."
Iris: "Welp, that's all from me. Is there anything you guys wanna ask?"
Kakeru: "Err... I think we've got the gist of it?"
Haruka: "Indeed. We have a means of defeating the Evil Eye's User now as well."
Iris: "Then I'm gonna go. If you need anything, just call for me."
Kakeru: "Yeah, gotcha."
Iris: "All right... I guess I should go back to being formal for the end at least."
Iris: "I sincerely apologize for pushing such a difficult task onto you. By all rights, I should be the one to rectify my own mistakes, but sadly I am incapable of doing so."
Iris: "I humbly request that you aid me."
*She puts her hands on the ground and bows deeply.*
*When she looks up again, the Stigma on her forehead is gone.*
Satsuki: "Bwuh... I'm tiiiired. My throat's dry... Why'd you have to talk so much, god?"
Haruka: "Are you still conscious when lending your body to Iris-san?"
Satsuki: "Oh yeah. Though everything's kinda hazy. I got most of what you guys were saying though."
Kakeru: "Umm, thank you very much. We'll... Well, I dunno if we'll be able to take care of everything, but we'll try."
Satsuki: "Hmm... Is this really something you guys have to do?"
Haruka: "Is there anyone else who can?"
Satsuki: "What you guys are doing is dangerous, right? Even the god said to prioritize your own safety."
Satsuki: "It's not like you have to stake your lives on this or anything. This isn't your responsibility."
Kakeru: "You've got a point, but..."
Kakeru: "..."
Kakeru: "Nah, I think this is my responsibility. Or at least there's a reason I have to see this through."
Kakeru: "Someone's gotta do it, so it may as well be me. In fact, I want it to be me."
Satsuki: "Ooooh, it's rare to see you so gung-ho about something."
Haruka: "That was a wonderful speech, Kakeru-sama."
Kakeru: "Err... Haha."
Satsuki: "This is kinda off topic, but you're making her call you Kakeru-sama? That's fucking creepy."
Haruka: "I call him that of my own volition. Ehehe."
Satsuki: "She's pretty different from last time, huh? Well, whatever."
Satsuki: "You better not do anything crazy. I'll do what I can to help, but not if it means putting you kids at risk."
Kakeru: "I know."
Satsuki: "I don't like that look on your face at all... Eh, I guess it's not a big deal. Actually it is, but whatever."
Satsuki: "You kids better go home. I'm beat."
Kakeru: "We will. Thanks again."
Haruka: "We appreciate your cooperation."
Satsuki: "Yeah yeah, good work and all that. Night, guys."
*With that half-assed sendoff, we leave the main hall.*
Kakeru: "..."
*Ambrosia, huh?*
*With this, we can put a stop to Yoichi's misdeeds.*
Haruka: "We may have obtained a trump card, but..."
Kakeru: "Yeah..."
Haruka: "Will you really use that medicine?"
Kakeru: "..."
Kakeru: "Let's tell everyone about Iris."
Haruka: "That will mean breaking our promise to her."
Kakeru: "I know, but I still think we should tell them. This decision is too important for us to make alone."
Kakeru: "We should decide as a group whether or not we're going to use this."
Haruka: "I see you still don't trust Iris-san."
Kakeru: "I did think she was more friendly and forthcoming than expected, but..."
Kakeru: "I guess I'm just paranoid. I couldn't see through Yoichi's true nature, so I'm scared of going with my gut now."
Kakeru: "Since I can't trust my own judgment, I think it's better to discuss this with everyone."
Haruka: "You'll hear no objections from me. In fact, I agree wholeheartedly."
Kakeru: "Then let's all meet up tomorrow."
Haruka: "Very well. It would be nice if this led to a breakthrough."
Kakeru: "Let's hope it does."
Haruka: "Indeed."
*Smiling, Senpai links arms with me.*
Haruka: "Would you be so kind as to see me to the station?"
Kakeru: "Uh, yeah, sure."
Haruka: "Oh my, do you not want to?"
Kakeru: "No, uhh... it's a little embarrassing, that's all."
Haruka: "Hehehe. Oh, you're so cute, Kakeru-sama. Come, let us be off."
Kakeru: "C-Cute?! O-Okay."
*Arm in arm, we head out of the shrine.*
*If we use this Ambrosia, it'll mean we trust Iris.*
*It also means we'll be betraying Sophie.*
*I... dunno how I feel about that.*
*Maybe Takamine was onto something when he gave me the codename Betrayer.*
*I need to weigh my options carefully.*
*I can't afford to make any more mistakes.*
*We can't let there be any more victims.*
*When I get back to my room I heave a tired sigh.*
*I left the Ambrosia with Senpai.*
*Sophie's usually monitoring me, so it's not a good idea for me to hang onto it.*
*That being said, shouldn't Sophie know that we're in touch with Iris by now?*
*Maybe she won't even contact us if she thinks we've betrayed her.*
*Just as I think that—*
Kakeru: "Ah."
*Sophie materializes above my table.*
*I'm instantly on my guard.*
Sophitia: "Have a minute?"
Kakeru: "Y-Yeah, I do."
Sophitia: "I see you've found another User."
Kakeru: "Huh? Oh, you mean Ms. Naruse."
Sophitia: "She's from the Naruse family, correct?"
Kakeru: "Yeah."
Sophitia: "I see. So the Eye chose her."
Kakeru: "Does the World's Eye only choose people from the Naruse family to be its User?"
Sophitia: "It does. It's a very special Artifact. So, are you getting any better at using it?"
Kakeru: "Uhh... I dunno. I still can't observe other branches like you can, Sophie."
Sophitia: "I see. That may be a side effect of the damage it received."
Kakeru: "Oh yeah, you said there's only a piece of it inside me, right?"
Sophitia: "That's nothing more than a theory. There may be a piece of it inside you, or there may not."
Sophitia: "Either way, it doesn't appear to have lost its power. The Eye is still functioning, albeit imperfectly."
Sophitia: "If she could master her power it would make my life a lot easier, but..."
Kakeru: "Ms. Naruse was pretty confused. She only just learned about her powers and Artifacts and all that."
Sophitia: "Well, I'll make sure not to expect too much."
*Sophie lets out a small sigh.*
*From the looks of it she hasn't realized Iris is borrowing Ms. Naruse's body from time to time.*
*She probably thinks we only went to Ms. Naruse's house to explain the situation to her.*
*At the very least, it's clear she didn't overhear our conversation.*
*The question is, should I tell her about it?*
*It's hard to be sure, but there is one thing I should confirm while she's here.*
Kakeru: "Umm, you came here to tell us how the hunt for Yoichi's going, right?"
Sophitia: "I did. Though I'm afraid the only report I have for you is that I haven't found him yet."
Kakeru: "...Can I ask you something?"
Sophitia: "What?"
Kakeru: "Is there a way to break someone's contract with their Artifact without killing them?"
Sophitia: "Of course there is."
Kakeru: "Can you tell me what it is? I don't want to kill Yoichi. Hell, if we try to fight him head on he'll probably end up killing us."
Sophitia: "There is a certain medicine that can cancel contracts. Though I can't imagine he'll just let you use it on him."
Kakeru: "What kind of medicine?"
Sophitia: "It's called Ambrosia. It puts whoever drinks it into a state of apparent death, fooling the Artifact into annulling its contract."
Kakeru: "Do you think... you could give us some of that?"
Sophitia: "I was planning to from the start, but I'm afraid I can't give you any right now."
Kakeru: "Why not?"
Sophitia: "Because I don't have any. I'm having a vial made as fast as possible, but it'll still take another half-month or so before its ready."
Kakeru: "Half a month... That's a long time."
Sophitia: "I feel the same way, but there's nothing I can do. I wasn't expecting to end up in a situation like this, so I didn't have any made ahead of time."
Sophitia: "At any rate, I'll give it to you once it's ready, so just be patient."
Kakeru: "Just be patient? You want me to sit still and do nothing?"
Sophitia: "Correct. It's not as if half a month of training will be enough to get you to that kid's level anyway."
Kakeru: "..."
Sophitia: "Huh. I expected you to be angrier. I guess even you're aware of the gap in power."
Kakeru: "Considering how badly we lost last time, I don't really have the right to argue. This isn't a shounen manga, we won't magically get ten times stronger just because we go through a training arc."
Sophitia: "I do have one piece of advice for you, though."
Kakeru: "Shoot."
Sophitia: "The key to drawing out an Artifact's power is your heart. Or to be more specific, the strength of your soul."
Sophitia: "The stronger your spirit, the stronger your Artifact will become."
Kakeru: "So we should focus on training our spirits over our bodies?"
Sophitia: "Pretty much. However..."
Kakeru: "However, what?"
Sophitia: "If you grow too fast, you'll end up destroying yourselves. Much like that boy who almost burned down your school."
Kakeru: "..."
Sophitia: "Which is why I've been telling you all to stay put and rest. Strength born from haste will bring only ruin. That I can guarantee."
Kakeru: "...Gotcha."
Sophitia: "Good, you're finally listening to reason. Granted, your Artifact isn't really meant for fighting so in your case it's probably better to train your body over your spirit. A bodybuilder physique would help you more than anything right now."
Kakeru: "No way I'm getting that buff in half a month."
*I smile ruefully and Sophie smiles back... I think.*
*...*
*Is she only showing concern for us as a way to gain our trust?*
*Is she actually egging Yoichi on from the shadows and pushing him to kill people for their Artifacts?*
*I have no idea who I should believe.*
Sophitia: "You're probably better off coming up with a plan for how you're going to force Yoichi to drink the Ambrosia rather than trying to train yourself anyway."
Kakeru: "Good point. I'll focus on that."
Sophitia: "Bye, then. If anything happens I'll let you know."
Kakeru: "Yeah, thanks."
*As soon as the words leave my mouth the space around Sophie warps and she returns to her world.*
*This conversation didn't do anything to ease my suspicions, but...*
*I'm pretty confident that Ambrosia Iris gave us is the real deal now.*
*The question is, how are we gonna get Yoichi to drink it?*
*...*
Kakeru: "There's no way we're gonna get him to drink it without fighting him first, huh?"
*Grumbling to myself, I flop onto my bed.*
*For now, I can at least say we made some progress.*
*We can work on a plan tomorrow when we all meet up.*

*We all gather at Nine Ball the first afternoon of Golden Week.*
*Senpai and I tell everyone about Iris, and the Ambrosia she gave us.*
Noa: "Can we trust her?"
Kakeru: "Well... That's the million dollar question."
*We don't know enough to be sure of whether she's trustworthy or not.*
*I haven't come to a conclusion myself, so there's no way I can answer Yuuki's question.*
*However...*
Kakeru: "Both Sophie and Iris have said this Ambrosia medicine can forcibly cancel someone's contract with their Artifact."
Kakeru: "So unless the two of them are secretly working together to confuse us, I think we can trust that this medicine works."
Sora: "If even a god's lying to us, is there anyone we CAN trust?"
Kakeru: "I get what you're saying, but we can't just blindly put our faith in her."
Miyako: "I never imagined the White Serpent who saved Shiromitsugawa a millennium ago would still be alive..."
Haruka: "She said she's stopped her aging."
Sora: "Damn, gods are amazing. Hang on, isn't this like one of those RPG developments where a goddess or whatever gives the hero an important quest item when they're in trouble?"
*Sora picks up the Ambrosia lying on the table and scrutinizes the vial.*
Kakeru: "You better not drop it."
Sora: "I know, I—Whoa!"
*The vial slips from Sora's fingers and drops back onto the table.*
Kakeru: "What did I just say?!"
Sora: "But I mean—look! There's this giant needle sticking out of it now!"
Miyako: "Oh, you're right. What's this needle for?"
Haruka: "Come to think of it, she did mention it could either be drunk or injected."
Sora: "Injected?! This needle's for injections?! You've gotta be kidding me! This is sharp enough to be a lethal weapon!"
Noa: "I suppose this could be considered Nal's thorn then."
Sora: "Hm? Wait, huh?"
Noa: "Loki's mother. She's also known as Laufey."
Sora: "I see. I still have no clue what you're talking about, but I see."
Miyako: "So it can be drunk or injected..."
*This time Kujo picks the vial up.*
Miyako: "If we manage to administer it via either method, we can forcibly cancel someone's contract with their Artifact?"
Kakeru: "Yeah. But the problem is..."
Sora: "How're we gonna make that happen?"
Kakeru: "Yep, exactly."
Noa: "Until we know our foe's location, we cannot hope to formulate a plan."
Sora: "Yeah, but we've gotta start somewhere. Let's try running some simulations at least. For example, if we wanna get him to drink it, how'd we go about doing that?"
Haruka: "We could slip it into his drink..."
Miyako: "That might work. But how would we do that without being noticed?"
Sora: "Think he'd show up if we invited him to a tea party?"
Kakeru: "Don't be stupid... is what I'd like to say, but knowing him he just might."
Noa: "He doesn't see us as a threat. Considering his personality, it's a possibility we can't rule out."

Kakeru: "Yeah. I actually texted him after our battle, but he didn't reply."
Sora: "But that means we can get in touch with him."
Kakeru: "Technically, yeah."
Sora: "So imagine this. You invite him to Nine Ball."
Kakeru: "Okay."
Sora: "He texts you back 'Sure, let's hang out.' Then he shows up here. All right Miya, you're up!"
Miyako: "Ah, okay."
Sora: "Since you're a part-timer here you can mix it into his drink while you're in the kitchen. Then you bring it to his table and he downs it without batting an eye."
Sora: "'Gaaaaah! I can feel my power leaving meeeeee!' There you go, we did it!"
Sora: "What do you guys think? Perfect, right?!"
Kakeru: "..."
Haruka: "..."
Miyako: "..."
Noa: "..."
Sora: "All right, all right, I get it. No need to say anything. Our assumptions were off. There's no way he'd accept an invitation just like that. Are you a moron or something, broski?"
Kakeru: "Screw you. You're the one who came up with that plan, not me."
Sora: "Also, like, it sounds like we're plotting to poison someone or something. It's not the kind of thing the good guys should be doing."
Kakeru: "Well... Yeah, but the only way we can beat him is through underhanded methods like this."

Haruka: "Yes."
Sora: "What about Commander?"
Haruka: "Ah, I think I can control him."
Miyako: "Could you make Commander give him the spiked drink?"

Sora: "This is totally the kind of thing the bad guys always try and pull."

Sophitia: "There's something I haven't told Kakeru, but I'll tell you everything."
Sophitia: "Iris was a researcher of magic. Not a mage, just a researcher. She didn't have any talent for magic, you see."
Sophitia: "She loved magic more than anyone, and understood it better than everyone else, but her lack of talent meant she couldn't handle using it."
Sophitia: "That's why her standing was so low, and as knowledgeable as she was, no one respected her for it. I'll even go so far as to say that she was loathed."
Sophitia: "They forced her to take on the bothersome job of collecting the Artifacts that leaked out of our world, because they saw her as a useless lackey."
Sophitia: "I'm sure you've asked yourself why Iris had to collect the Artifacts alone. Well, that's why."
Sophitia: "No one cared whether they plunged other worlds into chaos. So they ordered a single, low-ranking researcher to retrieve the Artifacts."
Sophitia: "Iris gained the Naruse family's assistance and retrieved the Artifacts. And that was her turning point."
Sophitia: "To Iris, who couldn't use any magic, the Artifacts were an alluring tool."
Sophitia: "And so she began to wonder. Did she really need to hand over the Artifacts she retrieved? Couldn't she make them her own?"
